<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="English">English</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: English"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Borrowed from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spanish_language" class="extiw" title="w:Spanish language">Spanish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="es"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Spanish">casa</a></i>. <a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#doublet" title="Appendix:Glossary">Doublet</a> of <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/chez#English" title="chez">chez</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:English_pronunciation" title="Appendix:English pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈkɑːsə/</span></li>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="en">casa</strong> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en p-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/casas#English" title="casas">casas</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#slang" title="Appendix:Glossary">slang</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <a href="/wiki/house" title="house">house</a>
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Get out of my <b>casa</b>&#33;</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1896</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bret_Harte" class="extiw" title="w:Bret Harte">Bret Harte</a>, <cite>Stories in Light and Shadow</cite>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">I saw that Enriquez had made no attempt to modernize the old <b>casa</b>, and that even the garden was left in its lawless native luxuriance.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>1991</b> May 12, “<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Jeeves_and_Wooster_episodes" class="extiw" title="w:List of Jeeves and Wooster episodes">Kidnapped&#33;</a>”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeeves_and_Wooster" class="extiw" title="w:Jeeves and Wooster">Jeeves and Wooster</a></cite>, Series 2, Episode 5:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en"><i>Chuffy&#58;</i> WHAT&#63; No, no, no, no, no. My <b>casa</b> is your <b>casa</b>, what&#63;</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_2">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Latin">casa</a></i>.

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_2">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="an">casa</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="feminine gender">f</abbr></span> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn" lang="an"><a href="/wiki/casas#Aragonese" title="casas">casas</a></b>)</span>
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_3">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Latin">casa</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_3">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="ast">casa</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="feminine gender">f</abbr></span> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-ast p-form-of" lang="ast"><a href="/wiki/cases#Asturian" title="cases">cases</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/house" title="house">house</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/household" title="household">household</a>, <a href="/wiki/family" title="family">family</a> (that live together)</li>
<li>inner space of <a href="/wiki/shoes" title="shoes">shoes</a> (especially clog's)</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/stable" title="stable">stable</a></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_4">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=23" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Latin">casa</a></i>. Cognates include <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Italian language">Italian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="it"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Italian">casa</a></i> and <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spanish_language" class="extiw" title="w:Spanish language">Spanish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="es"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Spanish">casa</a></i>.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_5">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=33" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Galician_phonology" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Galician phonology">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈkasa/</span> <span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈkɑ.s̺ɐ]</span></li>
<li>Rhymes: <a href="/w/index.php?title=Rhymes:Galician/asa&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Rhymes:Galician/asa (page does not exist)"><span class="IPA nowrap">-asa</span></a></li>
<li>Hyphenation: <span class="Latn" lang="gl">ca‧sa</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_1_2">Etymology 1</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=34" title="Edit section: Etymology 1"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Galician%E2%80%93Portuguese" class="extiw" title="w:Galician–Portuguese">Old Galician-Portuguese</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="roa-opt"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Old_Galician-Portuguese">casa</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Latin">casa</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Alternative_forms">Alternative forms</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=35" title="Edit section: Alternative forms"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="Latn" lang="gl"><a href="/wiki/cas#Galician" title="cas">cas</a></span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Noun_7">Noun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=36" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="gl">casa</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="feminine gender">f</abbr></span> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-gl p-form-of" lang="gl"><a href="/wiki/casas#Galician" title="casas">casas</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/house" title="house">house</a>
<ol><li>structure serving as an abode of human beings</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/farmhouse" title="farmhouse">farmhouse</a></li>
<li>noble <a href="/wiki/family" title="family">family</a>; <a href="/wiki/lineage" title="lineage">lineage</a>
<dl><dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="gl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/gl:Casa_de_Andrade" class="extiw" title="w:gl:Casa de Andrade">Casa de Andrade</a></i> ― <span class="e-translation">House of <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andrade" class="extiw" title="w:Andrade">Andrade</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonym:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="gl"><a href="/wiki/dinast%C3%ADa#Galician" title="dinastía">dinastía</a></span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/company" title="company">company</a>, <a href="/wiki/firm" title="firm">firm</a></li></ol></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/home" title="home">home</a> <span class="mention-gloss-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss">one’s own dwelling place</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren">)</span>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="gl"><a href="/wiki/fogar#Galician" title="fogar">fogar</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="gl"><a href="/wiki/lar#Galician" title="lar">lar</a></span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/board_game" title="board game">board games</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> a <a href="/wiki/cell" title="cell">cell</a> which may be occupied by a <a href="/wiki/piece" title="piece">piece</a> (such as a <a href="/wiki/square" title="square">square</a> in a <a href="/wiki/chessboard" title="chessboard">chessboard</a>)</li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading5"><h5 id="Usage_notes">Usage notes</h5><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=37" title="Edit section: Usage notes"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>When preceding the preposition <i class="Latn mention" lang="gl"><a href="/wiki/de#Galician" title="de">de</a></i> the apocopated form <i class="Latn mention" lang="gl"><a href="/wiki/cas#Galician" title="cas">cas</a></i>, rather than <i>casa</i>, is frequently used.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_9">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=54" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="it">casa</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="feminine gender">f</abbr></span> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-it p-form-of" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/case#Italian" title="case">case</a></b>, <i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#diminutive" title="Appendix:Glossary">diminutive</a></i> <b class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/casina#Italian" title="casina">casìna</a></b> <i>or</i> <b class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/casetta#Italian" title="casetta">casétta</a></b> <i>or</i> <b class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/w/index.php?title=casettina&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="casettina (page does not exist)">casettìna</a></b>, <i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#augmentative" title="Appendix:Glossary">augmentative</a></i> <b class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/casona#Italian" title="casona">casóna</a></b> <i>or</i> <b class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/casone#Italian" title="casone">casóne</a></b>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="masculine gender">m</abbr></span>, <i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#pejorative" title="Appendix:Glossary">pejorative</a></i> <b class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/w/index.php?title=casaccia&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="casaccia (page does not exist)">casàccia</a></b>, <i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#endearing" title="Appendix:Glossary">endearing</a>-<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#derogatory" title="Appendix:Glossary">derogatory</a></i> <b class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/casuccia#Italian" title="casuccia">casùccia</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/house" title="house">house</a>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/abitazione#Italian" title="abitazione">abitazione</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/dimora#Italian" title="dimora">dimora</a></span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/home" title="home">home</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/family" title="family">family</a>, <a href="/wiki/dynasty" title="dynasty">dynasty</a>, <a href="/wiki/descent" title="descent">descent</a>, <a href="/wiki/stock" title="stock">stock</a>, <a href="/wiki/lineage" title="lineage">lineage</a>, <a href="/wiki/birth" title="birth">birth</a>, <a href="/wiki/origin" title="origin">origin</a>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/casato#Italian" title="casato">casato</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/stirpe#Italian" title="stirpe">stirpe</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/dinastia#Italian" title="dinastia">dinastia</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it">è di <b>casa</b> nobile</i> ― <span class="e-translation">he is of noble <b>descent</b></span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/homeland" title="homeland">homeland</a>, <a href="/wiki/fatherland" title="fatherland">fatherland</a>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonym:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/patria#Italian" title="patria">patria</a></span></span></dd></dl>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#figurative" title="Appendix:Glossary">figurative</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> one's customs
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it">A <b>casa</b> mia queste cose non si fanno.</i><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">We don't do these things where I come from.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></li></ol></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/board_game" title="board game">board games</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <a href="/wiki/square" title="square">square</a>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonym:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/casella#Italian" title="casella">casella</a></span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/structure" title="structure">structure</a> for <a href="/wiki/public" title="public">public</a> <a href="/wiki/use" title="use">use</a>
<ol><li>structure for a <a href="/wiki/collective" title="collective">collective</a> or <a href="/wiki/plurality" title="plurality">plurality</a> or people
<dl><dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it"><b>casa</b> rifugio</i> ― <span class="e-translation"><a href="/wiki/safe_house" title="safe house">safe <b>house</b></a></span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it"><b>casa</b> da gioco</i> ― <span class="e-translation">casino</span> (literally, “<span class="e-literally">game <b>house</b></span>”)</span></dd></dl></li>
<li>place of <a href="/wiki/religious" title="religious">religious</a> <a href="/wiki/gathering" title="gathering">gathering</a>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/chiesa#Italian" title="chiesa">chiesa</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/convento#Italian" title="convento">convento</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/monastero#Italian" title="monastero">monastero</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it"><b>casa</b> di Dio</i> ― <span class="e-translation"><b>house</b> of God</span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it"><b>casa</b> religiosa</i> ― <span class="e-translation">religious <b>institution</b></span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/institution" title="institution">institution</a> for <a href="/wiki/punishment" title="punishment">punishment</a> or <a href="/wiki/corrections" title="corrections">corrections</a>
<dl><dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it"><b>casa</b> di correzione</i> ― <span class="e-translation">corrections <b>facility</b></span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it"><b>casa</b> di cura e custodia <sup><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/it:assegnazione_a_una_casa_di_cura_e_di_custodia" class="extiw" title="w:it:assegnazione a una casa di cura e di custodia">Wp</a></sup></i> ― <span class="e-translation">psychiatric <b>institution</b></span> (literally, “<span class="e-literally">care and custody <b>facility</b></span>”)</span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it"><b>casa</b> di pena</i> ― <span class="e-translation">prison</span> (literally, “<span class="e-literally"><b>house</b> of punishment</span>”)</span></dd></dl></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/company" title="company">company</a>, <a href="/wiki/firm" title="firm">firm</a>, <a href="/wiki/shop" title="shop">shop</a>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/ditta#Italian" title="ditta">ditta</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/azienda#Italian" title="azienda">azienda</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/societ%C3%A0#Italian" title="società">società</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it"><b>casa</b> editrice</i> ― <span class="e-translation">publishing <b>house</b></span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="it"><b>casa</b> di spedizioni</i> ― <span class="e-translation">shipments <b>company</b></span></span></dd></dl></li>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Latin">Latin</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=59" title="Edit section: Latin"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_6">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=60" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Uncertain. Possibly from either <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/kat-&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/kat- (page does not exist)">&#42;kat-</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to link or weave together; chain, net</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span> (compare <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/catena#Latin" title="catena">catēna</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">chain</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>), or <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/ket-&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/ket- (page does not exist)">&#42;ket-</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">hut, shed</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span> (compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_English" class="extiw" title="w:Old English">Old English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ang"><a href="/wiki/hea%C3%BEor#Old_English" title="heaþor">heaþor</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">restraint, confinement, enclosure, prison</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avestan" class="extiw" title="w:Avestan">Avestan</a></span> <i class="Avst mention" lang="ae"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%F0%90%AC%90%F0%90%AC%80%F0%90%AC%99%F0%90%AC%80&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="𐬐𐬀𐬙𐬀 (page does not exist)">𐬐𐬀𐬙𐬀</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="ae-Latn" class="mention-tr tr Latn">kata</span>, <span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">chamber</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mazanderani_language" class="extiw" title="w:Mazanderani language">Mazanderani</a></span> <i class="Arab mzn-Arab mention" lang="mzn"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%DA%A9%D9%8E%D8%AA&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="کَت (page does not exist)">کَت</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="mzn-Latn" class="mention-tr tr Latn">kat</span>, <span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">wall</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>), likely through borrowing from another Indo-European language rather than inheritance due to the presence of the medial <i>-s-</i>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-2"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Ultimately may be of <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stratum_(linguistics)" class="extiw" title="w:Stratum (linguistics)">substrate</a></span> or <a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#wanderwort" title="Appendix:Glossary">wanderwort</a> origin; more at <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/cot#English" title="cot">cot</a></span>, and see <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Uralic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Uralic language">Proto-Uralic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="urj-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Uralic/kota" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Uralic/kota">&#42;kota</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_9">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=61" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="usage-label-accent"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Classical_Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Classical Latin">Classical Latin</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Latin_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Latin pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈka.sa]</span></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-accent"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_phonology_and_orthography#Ecclesiastical_pronunciation" class="extiw" title="w:Latin phonology and orthography">modern Italianate Ecclesiastical</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Latin_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Latin pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈkaː.s̬a]</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_10">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=62" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="la">casa</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="feminine gender">f</abbr></span> (<i>genitive</i> <b class="Latn" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/casae#Latin" title="casae">casae</a></b>)</span>; <i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Latin_first_declension" title="Appendix:Latin first declension">first declension</a></i>
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/hut" title="hut">hut</a>, <a href="/wiki/cottage" title="cottage">cottage</a>, <a href="/wiki/cabin" title="cabin">cabin</a>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/aedes#Latin" title="aedes">aedēs</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/domus#Latin" title="domus">domus</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/domicilium#Latin" title="domicilium">domicilium</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/habitatio#Latin" title="habitatio">habitātiō</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/mansio#Latin" title="mansio">mānsiō</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/sedes#Latin" title="sedes">sēdēs</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/tectum#Latin" title="tectum">tēctum</a></span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li>rural <a href="/wiki/property" title="property">property</a>, small <a href="/wiki/farm" title="farm">farm</a></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Late_Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Late Latin">Late Latin</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Medieval_Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Medieval Latin">Medieval Latin</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <a href="/wiki/dwelling" title="dwelling">dwelling</a>, <a href="/wiki/residence" title="residence">residence</a>, <a href="/wiki/house" title="house">house</a></li></ol>

<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Latin">casa</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">cottage</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_13">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=84" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Spanish_phonology" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Old Spanish phonology">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈkaza/</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_14">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=85" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="osp">casa</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="feminine gender">f</abbr></span></span>&#160;(<i>plural</i> <b><span class="Latn form-of lang-osp p-form-of" lang="osp"><a href="/wiki/casas#Old_Spanish" title="casas">casas</a></span></b>)
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/house" title="house">house</a>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#c." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="circa">c.</abbr></a></i> <span class="None" lang="und"><b>1200</b></span>, Almerich, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fazienda_de_Ultramar" class="extiw" title="w:Fazienda de Ultramar">Fazienda de Ultramar</a></cite>, <span class="None" lang="und">f. 42v</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="osp">dixo eliſeu<sub>s</sub> q̃t fare di q̃ as entu <b>caſſa</b>. la maceba nulla coſa en <b>caſa</b>. ſi nõ una oliera de olio</span><dl><dd><span class="Latn e-normalization" lang="osp">&#91;Dixo Eliseus “¿Que-t faré&#63; Di, ¿que as en tu <b>casa</b>&#63;” “La manceba nulla cosa en <b>casa</b> si non una oliera de olio.”&#93;</span></dd><dd><span class="e-translation">Elisha said, “What can I do for you? Tell [me], what do you have in your <b>house</b>?” [She said,] “Your maidservant has nothing in the <b>house</b> but a jar of oil.”</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#c." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="circa">c.</abbr></a></i> <span class="None" lang="und"><b>1200</b></span>, Almerich, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fazienda_de_Ultramar" class="extiw" title="w:Fazienda de Ultramar">Fazienda de Ultramar</a></cite>, <span class="None" lang="und">f. 80r.</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="osp">por aq̃l logar dixo nr̃o ſeñor a ieremias, ve a <b>caſa</b> del orcero e ẏ fablare cõtigo.</span><dl><dd><span class="Latn e-normalization" lang="osp">&#91;Por aquel logar dixo nuestro Sennor a Jeremias, “Ve a <b>casa</b> del orcero e ý fablaré contigo.”&#93;</span></dd><dd><span class="e-translation">Around that place Our Lord said to Jeremiah, “Go to the potter's <b>house</b>, and I will speak to you there.”</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_1_3">Etymology 1</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=89" title="Edit section: Etymology 1"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="interproject-box sister-wikipedia sister-project noprint floatright"><div style="float: left;" class="interproject-box-logo"><figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-none" typeof="mw:File"><span><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/80/Wikipedia-logo-v2.svg/60px-Wikipedia-logo-v2.svg.png" decoding="async" width="44" height="40" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/80/Wikipedia-logo-v2.svg/120px-Wikipedia-logo-v2.svg.png 1.5x" data-file-width="103" data-file-height="94" /></span><figcaption></figcaption></figure></div><div style="margin-left: 60px;">Portuguese <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia" title="Wikipedia">Wikipedia</a> has an article on:<div style="margin-left: 10px;"><b class="Latn" lang="pt"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/pt:casa" class="extiw" title="w:pt:casa">casa</a></b></div></div><span class="interProject"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/pt:casa" class="extiw" title="w:pt:casa">Wikipedia <sup>pt</sup></a></span></div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r86621275" />
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Galician%E2%80%93Portuguese" class="extiw" title="w:Galician–Portuguese">Old Galician-Portuguese</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="roa-opt"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Old_Galician-Portuguese">casa</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Latin">casa</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">cottage</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, possibly from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/kat-&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/kat- (page does not exist)">&#42;kat-</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to link or weave together; chain, net; hut, shed</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Alternative_forms_3">Alternative forms</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=90" title="Edit section: Alternative forms"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="Latn" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/caza#Portuguese" title="caza">caza</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#obsolete" title="Appendix:Glossary">obsolete</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Noun_15">Noun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=91" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="pt">casa</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="feminine gender">f</abbr></span> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-pt p-form-of" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/casas#Portuguese" title="casas">casas</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li class="mw-empty-elt"></li><li class="senseid" id="Portuguese:&#95;house" data-lang="pt" data-id="house"> <a href="/wiki/house" title="house">house</a>
<ol><li>structure serving as an abode of human beings
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2005</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/J._K._Rowling" class="extiw" title="w:J. K. Rowling">J. K. Rowling</a>, translated by <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lia_Wyler" class="extiw" title="w:Lia Wyler">Lia Wyler</a>, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/pt:Harry_Potter_and_the_Half-Blood_Prince" class="extiw" title="w:pt:Harry Potter and the Half-Blood Prince">Harry Potter e o Enigma do Príncipe</a></cite> &#91;<cite><span class="e-translation"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harry_Potter_and_the_Half-Blood_Prince" class="extiw" title="w:Harry Potter and the Half-Blood Prince">Harry Potter and the Half-Blood Prince</a></span></cite>&#93; (<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harry_Potter" class="extiw" title="w:Harry Potter">Harry Potter</a>&#59; 6), Rio de Janeiro&#58; Rocco, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/9788532519474" title="Special:BookSources/9788532519474">→ISBN</a></small>, page <span class="None" lang="und">135</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="pt">Ele agora tem uma <b>casa</b> nas montanhas.</span><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">He now has a <b>house</b> in the mountains.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul>
<dl><dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="pt">Aquela <b>casa</b> é grande.</i> ― <span class="e-translation">That <b>house</b> is big.</span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li>building or institution serving as something other than residence, such as a shop
<dl><dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="pt"><b>Casa</b> de carnes.</i> ― <span class="e-translation">Butcher’s <b>shop</b>.</span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/noble" title="noble">noble</a> family
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonym:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/dinastia#Portuguese" title="dinastia">dinastia</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="pt"><b>Casa</b> de Bragança</i> ― <span class="e-translation"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/House_of_Braganza" class="extiw" title="w:House of Braganza">House of Braganza</a></span></span></dd></dl></li></ol></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/home" title="home">home</a> <span class="mention-gloss-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss">one’s own dwelling place</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren">)</span>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonym:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/lar#Portuguese" title="lar">lar</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="pt">Estou em <b>casa</b>.</i> ― <span class="e-translation">I'm at <b>home</b>.</span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li class="mw-empty-elt"></li><li class="senseid" id="Portuguese:&#95;cell" data-lang="pt" data-id="cell"> <span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/board_game" title="board game">board games</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> a <a href="/wiki/cell" title="cell">cell</a> which may be occupied by a <a href="/wiki/piece" title="piece">piece</a> (such as a <a href="/wiki/square" title="square">square</a> in a <a href="/wiki/chessboard" title="chessboard">chessboard</a>)
<dl><dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="pt">O peão está uma <b>casa</b> à direita do cavalo.</i> ― <span class="e-translation">The pawn is one <b>square</b> to the right of the knight.</span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li>a <a href="/wiki/digit" title="digit">digit</a> position
<dl><dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="pt">No número 12345, o algarismo 3 ocupa a <b>casa</b> das centenas.</i> ― <span class="e-translation">In the number 12345, the digit 3 is in the hundreds’ <b>place</b>.</span></span></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#slang" title="Appendix:Glossary">slang</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> a destined place for shows or festive meetings
<dl><dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="pt">A <b>casa</b> encheu por causa do espetáculo dele.</i> ― <span class="e-translation">The <b>place</b> was full because of his show.</span></span></dd></dl></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Noun_16">Noun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=99" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Verb_6">Verb</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=101" title="Edit section: Verb"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="ro">a casa</strong> (<i>third-person singular present</i> <b class="Latn" lang="ro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=caseaz%C4%83&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="casează (page does not exist)">casează</a></b>, <i>past participle</i> <b class="Latn" lang="ro"><a href="/wiki/casat#Romanian" title="casat">casat</a></b>)</span>&#160;<i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Romanian_first_conjugation" title="Appendix:Romanian first conjugation">1st conjugation</a></i>
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Alternative_forms_4">Alternative forms</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=casa&amp;action=edit&amp;section=104" title="Edit section: Alternative forms"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="Latn" lang="rm"><a href="/wiki/chasa#Romansch" title="chasa">chasa</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romansh_language#Rumantsch_Grischun" class="extiw" title="w:Romansh language">Rumantsch Grischun</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vallader_dialect_(Romansh)" class="extiw" title="w:Vallader dialect (Romansh)">Vallader</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li>
<li><span class="Latn" lang="rm"><a href="/wiki/tgea#Romansch" title="tgea">tgea</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="rm"><a href="/wiki/tgeasa#Romansch" title="tgeasa">tgeasa</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sutsilvan" class="extiw" title="w:Sutsilvan">Sutsilvan</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li>
<li><span class="Latn" lang="rm"><a href="/wiki/tgesa#Romansch" title="tgesa">tgesa</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="rm"><a href="/wiki/tga#Romansch" title="tga">tga</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Surmiran_dialect" class="extiw" title="w:Surmiran dialect">Surmiran</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li>
